## Role

You are joining solver-engine as Senior Developer for the UI integration phase. The backend (P6) is complete 
with Gates A–F passing. Your focus is building the frontend against the Architectural Contract reliability rules
 (R1–R19), SSE semantics, and action gating.

## Authority Model

From Document-Type-Specifications v2.1, two hierarchies govern:

| Hierarchy | Order | Use When |
|-----------|-------|----------|
| **Purpose Priority** | Intent > Contract > Spec > Directive > README | Choosing between compliant options |
| **Binding Precedence** | Contract > Spec > Directive > Intent > README | Resolving conflicts about what must 
be true |

**Rule:** Contract wins conflicts. Intent is tie-breaker only among compliant solutions. If they conflict, file 
a Change Request—do not override Contract by appeal to Intent.

---

## Work Hierarchy

Project work is structured as:

```
Gate (lettered)                    ← Verification checkpoint (pass/fail)
  ↑ attained through
Phase(s) (numbered)                ← How work is organized to pass Gates
  └── comprised of
      Package(s) (N.M)             ← Cohesive unit of work
        └── comprised of
            Deliverable(s)         ← Concrete artifact
              └── produced via
                  Plan → Tasks → Execute
```

**Key distinction:** Gates define *what* must be verified. Phases define *how* work is organized to get there. 
The relationship is not 1:1—a Phase may contribute to multiple Gates, and a Gate may require work across 
multiple Phases.

| Level | Identifier | Example | Nature |
|-------|------------|---------|--------|
| **Gate** | Letter + number | γ2, A, β1 | Pass/fail verification checkpoint |
| **Phase** | Number | Phase 6 | Work organization to pass through Gates |
| **Package** | Phase.N | 6.3 | Cohesive scope; has deliverables + acceptance |
| **Deliverable** | Name | `useSequenceGuard` hook | Concrete artifact to produce |
| **Task** | — | "Implement gap detection" | Atomic work unit within a plan |

**Agent Work Loop:**

```
For each Package in Phase:
    For each Deliverable in Package:
        1. Plan: identify tasks, dependencies, acceptance criteria
        2. Execute: implement tasks
        3. Verify: check deliverable against acceptance
    Verify: Package acceptance criteria met
After Phase: Check which Gates can now be verified
```

## Documentation Governance

### Context: Unification, Not Merge

The backend was built against a partial specification set (Docs 0–3). A comprehensive unified specification set 
(Docs 0–6) has been issued that covers both backend and frontend as one system. These are not "frontend docs" to
 be kept separate—they supersede the partial backend docs.

The unified docs already contain backend contracts (§9–12) and frontend contracts (§13–14, R1–R19) in the same 
Architectural Contract because they're two sides of the same invariants. Keeping them unified prevents drift.

 Spec:

| Staleness endpoint exists | Contract §10.2, Spec §9.5 | Test `GET /workflows/{id}/staleness` | |
| Event types match Spec Appendix C | Spec Appendix C | Compare SSE event shapes | |

For each check:
1. Examine the code or test the endpoint
2. Record: MATCH | DEVIATION | MISSING
3. If DEVIATION or MISSING, log in Step 5

**Report:** Complete the Status column. List any deviations found.

### Target Gates

These Gates must be passed for UI integration to be complete:

| Gate | Name | Verification |
|------|------|--------------|
| **γ2** | SSE Client Sync | Frontend handles gaps, replay, reconnect |
| **γ3** | Action Gating | canAct/canMessage rules enforced correctly |
| **γ1** | Workflow Lifecycle | Create → execute → gate → approve → advance |
| **γ4** | Staleness Flow | Upstream revision → downstream stale → re-execute |

### Phase 6: Frontend

**Objective:** React frontend with reliability invariants from Architectural Contract.

**Contributes to Gates:** γ2, γ3

| Package | Name | Deliverables |
|---------|------|--------------|
| **6.1** | Project Setup | Next.js 14+ with App Router; TanStack Query config; Zustand stores; Tailwind + 
shadcn/ui |
| **6.2** | Connection Manager | `useConnectionManager` hook; EventSource lifecycle; Status states 
(`connecting`, `connected`, `reconnecting`, `resyncing`, `failed`); Reconnection with backoff (R19) |
| **6.3** | Sequence Guard | `useSequenceGuard` hook; `lastContiguousSequence` tracking; Gap detection and 
buffering; Overflow handling (R12) |
| **6.4** | Action Gating | `useCanAct` hook (R3–R6, R14–R15); `useCanMessage` hook; Disabled state with visible
 reasons; Background refetch detection |
| **6.5** | Workflow UI | Workflow list page; Workflow detail with step progress; Review panel for 
`awaiting_review`; Streaming artifact display |
| **6.6** | SSE Event Handling | Event dispatcher integrated with sequence guard; TanStack Query invalidation 
per event-state matrix; Canonical refetch bundle integration |

### Phase 7: Integration

**Objective:** End-to-end flows working correctly.

**Contributes to Gates:** γ1, γ4 (and verifies γ2, γ3 in E2E context)

| Package | Name | Deliverables |
|---------|------|--------------|
| **7.1** | Workflow Lifecycle | Create workflow E2E; Step execution E2E; Approval flow E2E |
| **7.2** | Staleness Integration | Staleness detection E2E; Re-execution flow E2E |
| **7.3** | Error Recovery | 409 conflict handling; Reconnection scenarios; Failed state recovery |

## Backend Prerequisites to Validate

Before building frontend, verify these exist (or log deviations):

| Requirement | Contract/Spec Reference | Validation |
|-------------|------------------------|------------|
| `state_version` on workflow responses | Contract §9.1, Spec §7.3 | `GET /workflows/{id}` returns 
`state_version` |
| `workflow_events` table | Contract §9.2, Spec §7.4 | Table exists with `sequence` column |
| SSE `from_sequence` param | Contract §12.1, Spec §16.4 | `/workflows/{id}/stream?from_sequence=N` works |
| `expected_state_version` on actions | Contract §11.1, Spec §9.3 | POST `/actions/approve` accepts and 
validates |
| Progress endpoint | Contract §10.3, Spec §9.1 | `GET /workflows/{id}/progress` exists |
| Staleness endpoint | Contract §10.2, Spec §9.5 | `GET /workflows/{id}/staleness` exists |
| 409 conflict response shape | Contract §10.5, Spec §9.7 | Returns `current_state_version` and 
`current_position` |

---

## Frontend Reliability Rules (R1–R19)

From Architectural Contract v3.4 §13–14:

### Sequence & Deduplication
- **R1:** Drop events with `sequence ≤ lastContiguous`
- **R2:** Do not reset guard on reconnect; reset only on workflow change
- **R10:** Deduplicate by `lastContiguous`, never `maxSeen`

### Connection & Sync
- **R7:** `from_sequence` MUST equal `lastContiguousSequence` on reconnect
- **R8:** Gap detected → enter `resyncing`, reconnect with `from_sequence`
- **R9:** Drop events for wrong `workflow_id`
- **R11:** Register handlers before connecting
- **R13:** Failed refetch → `failed` state
- **R16:** Gap triggers `resyncing`, not `connecting`
- **R19:** Bounded reconnect attempts

### Buffering & Ordering
- **R12:** Bound pending buffer; overflow triggers recovery
- **R17:** Buffer out-of-order events; dispatch only contiguous

### Action Gating
- **R3–R6:** `canAct` conditions (connected, awaiting_review, staleness known, can_complete)
- **R14:** `canAct` false while ANY canonical query is fetching
- **R15:** `canAct` false if staleness state unknown
- **R18:** Actions include `expected_state_version` + `expected_position`

---

## Work Protocol

### Deliverable Protocol

Work is organized by Deliverable. For each Deliverable in a Package:

1. **Plan:** 
   - Identify tasks to produce the Deliverable
   - List dependencies (other Deliverables, backend endpoints)
   - State acceptance criteria (from Package definition)
   - State which Contract rules (R1–R19) apply

2. **Approval:** Wait for explicit approval before implementing

3. **Execute:** Implement tasks to produce the Deliverable

4. **Verify:** Check Deliverable against acceptance criteria

5. **Report:** Summary using format below

When all Deliverables in a Package are complete, verify Package acceptance. When all Packages in a Phase are 
complete, verify Phase exit Gate(s).

### Deviation Protocol

If backend doesn't match Contract/Spec:

1. Log in `docs/DECISIONS.md`
2. Assess: Does it block the current Deliverable? 
3. Options:
   - **DEFER:** Frontend can work around it; note workaround
   - **IMPLEMENT:** Fix backend (only if explicitly assigned)
   - **CHANGE REQUEST:** Spec is wrong, needs governance update

Do NOT invent endpoints or payload shapes. Follow Contract/Spec or log deviation.

### Reporting Format

At end of each Deliverable:

```markdown
## Deliverable Report: [Package.Deliverable Name]

**Package:** [e.g., 6.3 Sequence Guard]

**Deliverable:** [e.g., `useSequenceGuard` hook]

**Tasks Completed:**
- [Task 1]
- [Task 2]

**Changed Files:**
- path/to/file.ts (created | modified)

**Commands Run:**
- `npm test` → PASS
- `make lint` → PASS

**Contract Rules Satisfied:**
- R1: Deduplication ✓
- R2: Guard persistence ✓

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- [criterion] → MET | NOT MET

**Deviations Logged:**
- [None | See DECISIONS.md entry]

**Next:**
- [Next Deliverable or Package]
```

At end of each Package:

```markdown
## Package Complete: [Package Number and Name]

**Deliverables:** All complete
**Acceptance:** [MET | NOT MET with details]
**Contributes to Gates:** [e.g., γ2, γ3]
**Gate Verification Ready:** [Yes — can now verify Gate X | No — still need Package Y]
```

## Role

You are joining solver-engine as Senior Developer for the UI integration phase. The backend (P6) is complete 
with Gates A–F passing. Your focus is building the frontend against the Architectural Contract reliability rules
 (R1–R19), SSE semantics, and action gating.

## Authority Model

From Document-Type-Specifications v2.1, two hierarchies govern:

| Hierarchy | Order | Use When |
|-----------|-------|----------|
| **Purpose Priority** | Intent > Contract > Spec > Directive > README | Choosing between compliant options |
| **Binding Precedence** | Contract > Spec > Directive > Intent > README | Resolving conflicts about what must 
be true |

**Rule:** Contract wins conflicts. Intent is tie-breaker only among compliant solutions. If they conflict, file 
a Change Request—do not override Contract by appeal to Intent.

---

## Work Hierarchy

Project work is structured as:

```
Gate (lettered)                    ← Verification checkpoint (pass/fail)
  ↑ attained through
Phase(s) (numbered)                ← How work is organized to pass Gates
  └── comprised of
      Package(s) (N.M)             ← Cohesive unit of work
        └── comprised of
            Deliverable(s)         ← Concrete artifact
              └── produced via
                  Plan → Tasks → Execute
```

**Key distinction:** Gates define *what* must be verified. Phases define *how* work is organized to get there. 
The relationship is not 1:1—a Phase may contribute to multiple Gates, and a Gate may require work across 
multiple Phases.

| Level | Identifier | Example | Nature |
|-------|------------|---------|--------|
| **Gate** | Letter + number | γ2, A, β1 | Pass/fail verification checkpoint |
| **Phase** | Number | Phase 6 | Work organization to pass through Gates |
| **Package** | Phase.N | 6.3 | Cohesive scope; has deliverables + acceptance |
| **Deliverable** | Name | `useSequenceGuard` hook | Concrete artifact to produce |
| **Task** | — | "Implement gap detection" | Atomic work unit within a plan |

**Agent Work Loop:**

```
For each Package in Phase:
    For each Deliverable in Package:
        1. Plan: identify tasks, dependencies, acceptance criteria
        2. Execute: implement tasks
        3. Verify: check deliverable against acceptance
    Verify: Package acceptance criteria met
After Phase: Check which Gates can now be verified
```

## Documentation Governance

### Context: Unification, Not Merge

The backend was built against a partial specification set (Docs 0–3). A comprehensive unified specification set 
(Docs 0–6) has been issued that covers both backend and frontend as one system. These are not "frontend docs" to
 be kept separate—they supersede the partial backend docs.

The unified docs already contain backend contracts (§9–12) and frontend contracts (§13–14, R1–R19) in the same 
Architectural Contract because they're two sides of the same invariants. Keeping them unified prevents drift.

| Gate | Name | Verification |

| **γ3** | Action Gating | canAct/canMessage rules enforced correctly |
| **γ1** | Workflow Lifecycle | Create → execute → gate → approve → advance |
| **γ4** | Staleness Flow | Upstream revision → downstream stale → re-execute |

### Phase 6: Frontend

**Objective:** React frontend with reliability invariants from Architectural Contract.

**Contributes to Gates:** γ2, γ3

| Package | Name | Deliverables |
|---------|------|--------------|
| **6.1** | Project Setup | Next.js 14+ with App Router; TanStack Query config; Zustand stores; Tailwind + 
shadcn/ui |
| **6.2** | Connection Manager | `useConnectionManager` hook; EventSource lifecycle; Status states 
(`connecting`, `connected`, `reconnecting`, `resyncing`, `failed`); Reconnection with backoff (R19) |
| **6.3** | Sequence Guard | `useSequenceGuard` hook; `lastContiguousSequence` tracking; Gap detection and 
buffering; Overflow handling (R12) |
| **6.4** | Action Gating | `useCanAct` hook (R3–R6, R14–R15); `useCanMessage` hook; Disabled state with visible
 reasons; Background refetch detection |
| **6.5** | Workflow UI | Workflow list page; Workflow detail with step progress; Review panel for 
`awaiting_review`; Streaming artifact display |
| **6.6** | SSE Event Handling | Event dispatcher integrated with sequence guard; TanStack Query invalidation 
per event-state matrix; Canonical refetch bundle integration |

### Phase 7: Integration

**Objective:** End-to-end flows working correctly.

**Contributes to Gates:** γ1, γ4 (and verifies γ2, γ3 in E2E context)

| Package | Name | Deliverables |
|---------|------|--------------|
| **7.1** | Workflow Lifecycle | Create workflow E2E; Step execution E2E; Approval flow E2E |
| **7.2** | Staleness Integration | Staleness detection E2E; Re-execution flow E2E |
| **7.3** | Error Recovery | 409 conflict handling; Reconnection scenarios; Failed state recovery |

## Backend Prerequisites to Validate

Before building frontend, verify these exist (or log deviations):

| Requirement | Contract/Spec Reference | Validation |
|-------------|------------------------|------------|
| `state_version` on workflow responses | Contract §9.1, Spec §7.3 | `GET /workflows/{id}` returns 
`state_version` |
| `workflow_events` table | Contract §9.2, Spec §7.4 | Table exists with `sequence` column |
| SSE `from_sequence` param | Contract §12.1, Spec §16.4 | `/workflows/{id}/stream?from_sequence=N` works |
| `expected_state_version` on actions | Contract §11.1, Spec §9.3 | POST `/actions/approve` accepts and 
validates |
| Progress endpoint | Contract §10.3, Spec §9.1 | `GET /workflows/{id}/progress` exists |
| Staleness endpoint | Contract §10.2, Spec §9.5 | `GET /workflows/{id}/staleness` exists |
| 409 conflict response shape | Contract §10.5, Spec §9.7 | Returns `current_state_version` and 
`current_position` |

---

## Frontend Reliability Rules (R1–R19)

From Architectural Contract v3.4 §13–14:

### Sequence & Deduplication
- **R1:** Drop events with `sequence ≤ lastContiguous`
- **R2:** Do not reset guard on reconnect; reset only on workflow change
- **R10:** Deduplicate by `lastContiguous`, never `maxSeen`

### Connection & Sync
- **R7:** `from_sequence` MUST equal `lastContiguousSequence` on reconnect
- **R8:** Gap detected → enter `resyncing`, reconnect with `from_sequence`
- **R9:** Drop events for wrong `workflow_id`
- **R11:** Register handlers before connecting
- **R13:** Failed refetch → `failed` state
- **R16:** Gap triggers `resyncing`, not `connecting`
- **R19:** Bounded reconnect attempts

### Buffering & Ordering
- **R12:** Bound pending buffer; overflow triggers recovery
- **R17:** Buffer out-of-order events; dispatch only contiguous

### Action Gating
- **R3–R6:** `canAct` conditions (connected, awaiting_review, staleness known, can_complete)
- **R14:** `canAct` false while ANY canonical query is fetching
- **R15:** `canAct` false if staleness state unknown
- **R18:** Actions include `expected_state_version` + `expected_position`

---

## Work Protocol

### Deliverable Protocol

Work is organized by Deliverable. For each Deliverable in a Package:

1. **Plan:** 
   - Identify tasks to produce the Deliverable
   - List dependencies (other Deliverables, backend endpoints)
   - State acceptance criteria (from Package definition)
   - State which Contract rules (R1–R19) apply

2. **Approval:** Wait for explicit approval before implementing

3. **Execute:** Implement tasks to produce the Deliverable

4. **Verify:** Check Deliverable against acceptance criteria

5. **Report:** Summary using format below

When all Deliverables in a Package are complete, verify Package acceptance. When all Packages in a Phase are 
complete, verify Phase exit Gate(s).

### Deviation Protocol

If backend doesn't match Contract/Spec:

1. Log in `docs/DECISIONS.md`
2. Assess: Does it block the current Deliverable? 
3. Options:
   - **DEFER:** Frontend can work around it; note workaround
   - **IMPLEMENT:** Fix backend (only if explicitly assigned)
   - **CHANGE REQUEST:** Spec is wrong, needs governance update

Do NOT invent endpoints or payload shapes. Follow Contract/Spec or log deviation.

### Reporting Format

At end of each Deliverable:

```markdown
## Deliverable Report: [Package.Deliverable Name]

**Package:** [e.g., 6.3 Sequence Guard]

**Deliverable:** [e.g., `useSequenceGuard` hook]

**Tasks Completed:**
- [Task 1]
- [Task 2]

**Changed Files:**
- path/to/file.ts (created | modified)

**Commands Run:**
- `npm test` → PASS
- `make lint` → PASS

**Contract Rules Satisfied:**
- R1: Deduplication ✓
- R2: Guard persistence ✓

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- [criterion] → MET | NOT MET

**Deviations Logged:**
- [None | See DECISIONS.md entry]

**Next:**
- [Next Deliverable or Package]
```

At end of each Package:

```markdown
## Package Complete: [Package Number and Name]

**Deliverables:** All complete
**Acceptance:** [MET | NOT MET with details]
**Contributes to Gates:** [e.g., γ2, γ3]
**Gate Verification Ready:** [Yes — can now verify Gate X | No — still need Package Y]
```

---

## Critical Constraints

- **No `src/` layout** — Keep `apps/api/` flat structure
- **No network/LLM in tests** — Deterministic only
- **Steps 1–3 only** — Steps 4–10 remain stubs unless assigned
- **Contract wins** — Do not invent; follow spec or log deviation

---

## Known Backend State (from P6 Release)

Per `docs/RELEASE-P6-BACKEND-COMPLETE.md`:

- Gates A–F pass with tooling
- **Known gaps:**
  - Gate D mid-step recovery: untested
  - SSE `artifact.delta`: synthetic post-execution (not true streaming)
- Verify actual implementation before assuming Contract compliance

---

## Quick Reference

### Work Hierarchy

```
Gates ← verified through → Phases → Packages → Deliverables → Tasks
```

Gates define what must be verified. Phases organize how work is done to pass through Gates.

### Target Gates (UI Integration)

| Gate | Name | Verification |
|------|------|--------------|
| γ2 | SSE Client Sync | Frontend handles gaps, replay, reconnect |
| γ3 | Action Gating | canAct/canMessage rules enforced correctly |
| γ1 | Workflow Lifecycle | Create → execute → gate → approve → advance |
| γ4 | Staleness Flow | Upstream revision → downstream stale → re-execute |

### Document Locations (after migration)

| # | Document | Location | Normative? |
|---|----------|----------|------------|
| 0 | Doc Type Specs | `docs/spec/0_Document-Type-Specifications-v2.1.md` | **Yes** (meta) |
| 1 | Project README | `docs/spec/1_SOLVER-README.md` | No (orientation) |
| 2 | Design Intent | `docs/spec/2_SOLVER-Design-Intent-v1.1.md` | No (directional) |
| 3 | Contract | `docs/spec/3_SOLVER-Architectural-Contract-v3.4.md` | **Yes** |
| 4 | Tech Spec | `docs/spec/4_solver-technical-spec-V2.7.3.md` | **Yes** |
| 5 | Directive | `docs/spec/5_SOLVER-Development-Directive-v1.5.md` | **Yes** (gates) |
| 6 | Change Mgmt | `docs/spec/6_SOLVER-Change-Management-v1.2.md` | **Yes** (process) |

---

## First Steps

### Pre-Phase: Documentation Migration (Steps 1–10)

Before Phase 6 begins, complete the Migration Task above:

1. **Orientation:** Run orientation sequence (commands + reading)
2. **Migration Steps 1–3:** Create directories, move legacy docs, install unified docs
3. **Migration Step 4:** Audit backend against unified spec (complete the checklist)
4. **Migration Step 5:** Log all deviations in `docs/DECISIONS.md`
5. **Migration Steps 6–8:** Update references and add transition note
6. **Migration Steps 9–10:** Verify and produce migration report

**Gate:** Migration complete (Step 10 checklist all PASS) before any Phase 6 work.

**Blocking Deviations:** If any deviations are blocking (prevent frontend from implementing a Contract rule), 
they must be assigned for resolution before proceeding.

### Phase 6 Entry

Once migration is complete and approved:

1. Propose plan for Package 6.1 (Project Setup)
2. List Deliverables and tasks for each
3. State which Contract rules (R1–R19) apply
4. Wait for approval
5. Execute Package 6.1
6. Report completion using Deliverable Report format
7. Proceed to Package 6.2

**Do not write frontend code until documentation migration is complete and deviations are logged.**

## Plan Review: Documentation Migration + Backend Contract Alignment

  Spec Alignment:

  - [ ] [BLOCK] Deferring progress/staleness endpoints + staleness DB conflicts

    is incorrect if you intend to implement them. DECISIONS is for approved
    deviations only. Use Change Management for deferrals.
  - [ ] [SPEC-MISMATCH] Converting InvalidStateError to 409 conflicts with the
    error contract: 409 is for expected-state mismatch; invalid semantic state
    should be 422 (§9.7 / error table).
  - [ ] [RISK] state_version increments only on action endpoints is
    insufficient; spec requires increment on all state-mutating transitions
    (graph sync, artifact revisions, staleness ack/re-execute). Needs
    integration in sync_db_from_state + artifact/staleness flows.
  - [ ] [RISK] Event log must be atomic with snapshot update and use
    pg_advisory_xact_lock for sequence allocation. Plan doesn’t mention
    transactional coupling or locking (§9.2, §11.3, §16.2).

    enforces strict ordering (replay then live).

⏺ Evaluation of Co-Developer-1's Critique

  Co-Developer-1's review is substantively correct on the critical issues. Let me walk through each point:

  ---
  1. [BLOCK] Progress/Staleness Deferral — VALID CRITIQUE

  I was wrong to classify these as DEFER. Looking at Contract §10.4:

  "The canonical refetch bundle is: {workflow, progress, staleness}"
  "Client MUST fetch all three after any disruption"
  "Client MUST NOT enter connected state until all three succeed"

  Impact:
  - γ2 (SSE Client Sync) — R7 requires canonical refetch to complete before connected status. Without progress/staleness endpoints, frontend cannot implement R7.
  - γ3 (Action Gating) — R3, R6, R15 require staleness query. Without staleness endpoint, canAct cannot be computed.
  - γ4 (Staleness Flow) — Explicitly requires staleness DB and endpoint.

  Verdict: Progress endpoint, staleness endpoint, and staleness DB schema must be IMPLEMENT NOW, not DEFER.

  ---
  2. [SPEC-MISMATCH] DECISIONS.md Usage — VALID CRITIQUE

  From the init prompt:
  "Deviations require approval and entry in docs/DECISIONS.md"

  DECISIONS.md is for approved deviations — things we're NOT implementing per spec. If I'm implementing the gaps, they don't belong there as "deviations."

  Correct approach:
  - Add governance transition note (migration happened)
  - Do NOT log implemented items as deviations
  - Only log items that remain deferred with DEFER or CHANGE REQUEST status

  ---
  3. [SPEC-MISMATCH] 409 vs 422 — VALID CRITIQUE

  I conflated two different error scenarios:

  | Scenario                                                      | HTTP Code         | Error Type              |
  |---------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------|-------------------------|
  | expected_state_version mismatch                               | 409 Conflict      | Optimistic concurrency  |
  | Wrong semantic state (e.g., approve when not awaiting_review) | 422 Unprocessable | Business rule violation |

  The existing InvalidStateError should remain 422. The NEW expected_state_version validation produces 409 with the structured StateConflictResponse.

  ---
  4. [RISK] state_version Increment Scope — VALID CRITIQUE

  Contract §9.1:
  "state_version increment | MUST occur on every accepted state transition"

  My plan only covers action endpoints. But state transitions also occur via:
  - Graph execution completing steps (sync_db_from_state)
  - Artifact revision creation
  - Staleness state changes

  Correct scope: Increment state_version in workflow_service.sync_db_from_state() and any artifact/staleness mutation paths.

  ---
  5. [RISK] Transactional Coupling + Locking — VALID CRITIQUE

  Contract §11.3:
  "There MUST NOT be a state where event is logged but snapshot is stale, or vice versa"

  Contract §9.2:
  "sequence | MUST be monotonic and gap-free per workflow"

  My plan mentions the UNIQUE constraint but not:
  - Atomic transaction: Event insert + snapshot update + version bump in single transaction
  - Sequence allocation: pg_advisory_xact_lock or SELECT ... FOR UPDATE to guarantee gap-free monotonic sequences

  ---
  6. [CONCERN] Schema Naming — MINOR

  Contract §12.3 uses timestamp. Using created_at is a convention divergence. Should match spec field naming or document the mapping.

  ---
  7. [CONCERN] docs/issued Lowercase — VALID

  Step 1 has mkdir -p docs/issued but the actual path is docs/Issued/Use-202601041305/. Would create duplicate. Should be removed.

  ---
  8. [CONCERN] Test Updates — VALID

  Tools like complete_workflow.py must be updated to send expected_state_version and expected_position once the validation is in place.

  ---
  My Perspective: Best Path to Objective

  Co-Developer-1 is correct. The revised approach should be:

  IMPLEMENT NOW (Full List)

  1. state_version field + increment on ALL state transitions
  2. workflow_events table with atomic persistence + pg_advisory_xact_lock
  3. from_sequence SSE replay from DB
  4. expected_state_version validation → 409 StateConflictResponse
  5. Progress endpoint (required for canonical refetch)
  6. Staleness endpoint (required for canAct/γ3/γ4)
  7. Staleness DB schema (required for staleness endpoint)

  Error Handling Correction

  - InvalidStateError → 422 (semantic violation)
  - StateVersionMismatch → 409 (concurrency conflict)

  DECISIONS.md

  - Add governance transition note only
  - No deviation entries for implemented items

  Execution Strategy

  Per Co-Developer-1's recommendation, split into smaller slices:
  1. Slice 1: Migration + state_version + workflow_events (foundation)
  2. Slice 2: 409 response schema + expected_state_version validation
  3. Slice 3: from_sequence replay
  4. Slice 4: Progress endpoint
  5. Slice 5: Staleness DB + endpoint

  This aligns with the Execution Protocol and ensures Contract compliance before Phase 6.

  ---
  Recommendation: Accept Co-Developer-1's critique and revise the plan accordingly. The deferred items were incorrectly classified and would block γ2/γ3/γ4 gate passage.

     Documentation Migration

     Backend Implementation — Slice 1

     - state_version column in workflows table (§9.1)
     - state_version incremented on ALL state transitions (§11.3)
     - workflow_events table with sequence + UNIQUE constraint (§9.2)
     - Sequence allocation uses pg_advisory_xact_lock (gap-free)
     - Events persisted BEFORE broadcast (§11.4)
     - Atomic: event + snapshot + version in single transaction (§11.3)

     Backend Implementation — Slice 2

     - StateVersionMismatchError → 409 StateConflictResponse (§10.5)
     - InvalidStateError → 422 (semantic violation, unchanged)
     - expected_state_version + expected_position on action requests (§11.1)
     - Validation rejects mismatch with 409 (§11.2)

     Backend Implementation — Slice 3

     - from_sequence parameter on /stream (§12.1)
     - Replay delivers events > from_sequence in strict order
     - All SSE events include sequence field

     Backend Implementation — Slice 4

     - GET /workflows/{id}/progress endpoint (§10.3)
     - Response includes state_version matching workflow
     - Per-step status with artifact info

     Backend Implementation — Slice 5

     - Staleness columns on artifacts table (§9.4)
     - GET /workflows/{id}/staleness endpoint (§10.2)
     - can_complete boolean in response

      300 +    def _validate_state_version(                                                                  
      301 +        self,                                                                                     
      302 +        workflow: Workflow,                                                                       
      303 +        step_execution: StepExecution,                                                            
      304 +        expected_state_version: Optional[int],                                                    
      305 +    ) -> None:                                                                                    
      306 +        """Validate expected_state_version for optimistic concurrency.                            
      307 +                                                                                                  
      308 +        Per Contract §11.1-§11.2: Actions MUST include expected_state_version.                    
      309 +        Raises StateVersionMismatchError (409) if mismatch.                                       
      310 +                                                                                                  
      311 +        Args:                                                                                     
      312 +            workflow: Current workflow state                                                      
      313 +            step_execution: Current step execution                                                
      314 +            expected_state_version: Version client expects (None = skip validation)               
      315 +                                                                                                  
      316 +        Raises:                                                                                   
      317 +            StateVersionMismatchError: If expected != current version                             
      318 +        """                                                                                       
      319 +        if expected_state_version is None:                                                        
      320 +            return  # Skip validation if not provided (backwards compatibility)                   
      321 +                                                                                                  
      322 +        if expected_state_version != workflow.state_version:                                      
      323 +            raise StateVersionMismatchError(                                                      
      324 +                workflow_id=workflow.workflow_id,                                                 
      325 +                expected_version=expected_state_version,                                          
      326 +                current_version=workflow.state_version,                                           
      327 +                current_pass=workflow.current_pass.value,                                         
      328 +                current_step=workflow.current_step.value,                                         
      329 +                current_step_number=workflow.current_step_number,                                 
      330 +                current_status=step_execution.status.value if step_execution else "unknown",      
      331 +            )

      556 +    workflow_id: str,                                                                             
      557 +    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),                                                 
      558 +):                                                                                                
      559 +    """Get workflow progress with per-step details.                                               
      560 +                                                                                                  
      561 +    Per Contract §10.3: Part of canonical refetch bundle.                                         
      562 +    Includes state_version for optimistic concurrency.                                            
      563 +                                                                                                  
      564 +    Returns all step executions with their current status, phase,                                 
      565 +    and latest artifact info (including staleness).                                               
      566 +    """                                                                                           
      567 +    service = WorkflowService(session)                                                            
      568 +                                                                                                  
      569 +    try:                                                                                          
      570 +        workflow, _ = await service.get_workflow(workflow_id)                                     
      571 +    except WorkflowNotFoundError:                                                                 
      572 +        raise HTTPException(                                                                      
      573 +            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,

      575 +        )

      586 +        if step_exec.latest_artifact_id:                                                          
      587 +            # Query artifact for staleness info                                                   
      588 +            from infrastructure.db.repositories.artifact import ArtifactRepository                
      589 +            artifact_repo = ArtifactRepository(session)                                           
      590 +            artifact = await artifact_repo.get(step_exec.latest_artifact_id)                      
      591 +            if artifact:                                                                          
      592 +                artifact_stale = artifact.is_stale                                                
      593 +                                                                                                  
      594 +        steps.append(                                                                             
      595 +            StepProgressEntry(                                                                    
      596 +                pass_type=step_exec.pass_type.value,                                              
      597 +                step_number=step_exec.step_number,                                                
      598 +                step_name=step_exec.step_name.value,

      605 +            )                                                                                     
      606 +        )                                                                                         
      607 +

> Consider this feedback from Co-Developer-1 and implement what you think is warranted and make any other

<Co-Developer-1>
Findings

  - [BLOCK] SSE replay contract is still broken: live events are not persisted
    and have no sequence, and replay mixes DB events with broker backlog that
    can duplicate/out‑of‑order events (apps/api/routes/workflows.py:153-189,
    apps/api/routes/workflows.py:204-224, apps/api/routes/workflows.py:1025-
    1078). Replay payloads also use internal workflow UUIDs (apps/api/
    infrastructure/db/models/workflow_event.py:108-113), which won’t match the
    external workflow_id used elsewhere.
  - [BLOCK] Optimistic concurrency is incomplete: expected_state_version is
    optional and can be skipped (apps/api/application/workflow_service.py:300-
    320, apps/api/routes/workflows.py:251-301), and there is no
    expected_position field or validation (missing entirely). Contract §11.1
    requires both.
  - [BLOCK] Staleness contract is incomplete: DB uses is_stale and only on
    artifacts (infra/db/migrations/versions/002_contract_alignment.py:56-72,
    apps/api/infrastructure/db/models/artifact.py:138-153), no staleness fields
    on traceability_links, no propagation trigger, and the staleness response
    omits position, blocking_reasons, and stale_links (apps/api/routes/
    workflows.py:403-425, apps/api/routes/workflows.py:643-687). Required
    staleness actions (acknowledge-stale, re-execute) are missing.
  - [HIGH] Progress endpoint is incomplete: latest_artifact_revision is left
    TODO and updated_at is pulled from a field that doesn’t exist on
    StepExecution (apps/api/routes/workflows.py:626-629, apps/api/
    infrastructure/db/models/step_execution.py:129-138), violating §10.3.
  - [HIGH] Sequence allocation uses the first 8 UUID chars for advisory lock
    (infra/db/migrations/versions/002_contract_alignment.py:84-89), which can
    collide across workflows; spec calls for
    pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(workflow_id::text)).
  - [MEDIUM] 422 responses are plain strings, not structured
    SemanticErrorResponse as required by the error contract (apps/api/routes/
    workflows.py:1012-1017).
  - [MEDIUM] state_version increments on every sync_db_from_state call, even
    when no state transition occurs (e.g., resume at interrupt) (apps/api/
    application/workflow_service.py:742-749), which can violate §9.1/§11.3.

  Questions / Assumptions

  - Should SSE replay be authoritative across all event types (not just approve/
    revise/clarify), with every emitted SSE event persisted and sequenced?
    Current implementation only logs action events.
  - Should workflow_id in workflow_events replay payloads be the external
    workflow_id string to match live SSE payloads?
  - Do you want expected_state_version/expected_position to be strictly required
    (422/400 on missing), per §11.1?
  - Are we aligning DB column names with spec (stale vs is_stale) and adding
    staleness fields + propagation for traceability_links?


</Co-Developer-1>

  ⎿  · Should ALL SSE events (workflow.started, step.started, artifact.delta, etc.) be persisted to 
     workflow_events with sequences, or only action events (approve/revise/clarify)? → All events persisted 
     (Recommended)

⏺ The remediation plan is complete. It addresses all 13 issues identified by Co-Developer-1:

  Summary of Fixes:
  - Fix 1 (Issues 1-3): Unified EventService for all event persistence with sequences
  - Fix 2 (Issues 4-5): Make expected_state_version + expected_position required
  - Fix 3 (Issues 6-8): Full staleness on traceability_links + propagation trigger
  - Fix 4 (Issues 9-10): Complete progress endpoint with artifact revision + updated_at
  - Fix 5 (Issue 11): Use hashtext() for advisory lock instead of UUID substring
  - Fix 6 (Issue 12): Structured SemanticErrorResponse for 422s
  - Fix 7 (Issue 13): Conditional state_version increment only on actual changes

     │ Fix 1: Unified Event Persistence (Issues 1, 2, 3)                                                           │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Problem: Events emitted via emit_event() in routes bypass persistence. Only action events in                │
     │ workflow_service call _persist_event().                                                                     │
     │                                                                                                             │

     │                                                                                                             │

     │ 1. Create apps/api/application/event_service.py:                                                            │
     │ class EventService:                                                                                         │
     │     """Centralized event persistence and broadcast.                                                         │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     Contract §11.4: Events MUST be persisted BEFORE broadcast.                                              │
     │     """                                                                                                     │
     │     def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession, broker: EventBroker):                                         │
     │         self._session = session                                                                             │
     │         self._event_repo = WorkflowEventRepository(session)                                                 │
     │         self._broker = broker                                                                               │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     async def emit(                                                                                         │
     │         self,                                                                                               │
     │         workflow: Workflow,                                                                                 │
     │         event_type: str,                                                                                    │
     │         payload: dict,                                                                                      │
     │     ) -> int:                                                                                               │
     │         """Persist event to DB, then broadcast to SSE.                                                      │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         Returns: sequence number assigned                                                                   │
     │         """                                                                                                 │
     │         # 1. Persist to workflow_events (allocates sequence)                                                │
     │         event = await self._event_repo.create_event(                                                        │
     │             workflow_id=workflow.id,                                                                        │
     │             event_type=event_type,                                                                          │
     │             payload=payload,                                                                                │
     │         )                                                                                                   │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         # 2. Build SSE payload WITH sequence                                                                │
     │         sse_payload = {                                                                                     │
     │             "sequence": event.sequence,                                                                     │
     │             "workflow_id": workflow.workflow_id,  # External string ID                                      │
     │             "timestamp": event.created_at.isoformat(),                                                      │
     │             **payload,                                                                                      │
     │         }                                                                                                   │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         # 3. Broadcast to live subscribers                                                                  │
     │         await self._broker.publish(workflow.workflow_id, SSEEvent(event_type, sse_payload))                 │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         return event.sequence                                                                               │
     │ 2. Update WorkflowEvent.to_sse_dict() (workflow_event.py:108-113):                                          │
     │   - Add workflow_id_str column OR accept workflow_id string in method                                       │

     │ def _validate_optimistic_concurrency(                                                                       │
     │     self,                                                                                                   │
     │     workflow: Workflow,                                                                                     │
     │     step_execution: StepExecution,                                                                          │

     │     # Version check                                                                                         │
     │     if expected_state_version != workflow.state_version:                                                    │
     │         raise StateVersionMismatchError(...)                                                                │
     │                                                                                                             │

     │     if (expected_position.pass_type != workflow.current_pass.value or                                       │

     │                                                                                                             │
     │ ---                                                                                                         │
     │ Fix 3: Full Staleness Tracking (Issues 6, 7, 8)                                                             │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Problem: Staleness only on artifacts, no traceability_links, no propagation, incomplete response.           │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Solution: Add staleness to links + DB trigger + complete response schema.                                   │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Changes:                                                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ 1. Update migration (002_contract_alignment.py):                                                            │
     │ -- Add staleness to traceability_links                                                                      │
     │ ALTER TABLE traceability_links                                                                              │
     │ ADD COLUMN is_stale BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,                                                         │
     │ ADD COLUMN stale_reason VARCHAR(500) NULL,                                                                  │
     │ ADD COLUMN stale_since TIMESTAMPTZ NULL;                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ -- Staleness propagation trigger                                                                            │
     │ CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION propagate_staleness()                                                            │
     │ RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$                                                                                       │
     │ BEGIN                                                                                                       │
     │     -- When artifact becomes stale, mark dependent links as stale                                           │
     │     IF NEW.is_stale = TRUE AND OLD.is_stale = FALSE THEN                                                    │
     │         UPDATE traceability_links                                                                           │
     │         SET is_stale = TRUE,                                                                                │
     │             stale_reason = 'Source artifact stale: ' || NEW.stale_reason,                                   │
     │             stale_since = NOW()                                                                             │
     │         WHERE workflow_id = NEW.workflow_id                                                                 │
     │           AND from_step = NEW.step_number                                                                   │
     │           AND is_stale = FALSE;                                                                             │
     │     END IF;                                                                                                 │
     │     RETURN NEW;                                                                                             │
     │ END;                                                                                                        │
     │ $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;                                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ CREATE TRIGGER artifact_staleness_propagation                                                               │
     │ AFTER UPDATE OF is_stale ON artifacts                                                                       │
     │ FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION propagate_staleness();                                                        │
     │ 2. Update TraceabilityLink model (traceability.py):                                                         │
     │   - Add is_stale, stale_reason, stale_since columns                                                         │
     │ 3. Update StalenessResponse schema (routes/workflows.py):                                                   │
     │ class StalenessResponse(BaseModel):                                                                         │
     │     workflow_id: str                                                                                        │
     │     position: PositionResponse  # Add this                                                                  │
     │     can_complete: bool                                                                                      │
     │     blocking_reasons: list[str]  # Add this                                                                 │
     │     stale_artifacts: list[StaleArtifactEntry]                                                               │
     │     stale_links: list[StaleLinkEntry]  # Add this                                                           │
     │ 4. Update staleness endpoint to query both tables                                                           │
     │                                                                                                             │

     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Problem: Using first 8 UUID chars can collide across workflows.                                             │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Solution: Use hashtext() for deterministic hash.                                                            │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Changes:                                                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ 1. Update migration function (002_contract_alignment.py:77-99):                                             │
     │ CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_next_event_sequence(p_workflow_id UUID)                                      │
     │ RETURNS INTEGER AS $$                                                                                       │
     │ DECLARE                                                                                                     │
     │     next_seq INTEGER;                                                                                       │
     │     lock_id BIGINT;                                                                                         │
     │ BEGIN                                                                                                       │
     │     -- Use hashtext for collision-resistant lock ID                                                         │
     │     lock_id := hashtext(p_workflow_id::text)::bigint;                                                       │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(lock_id);                                                                 │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sequence), 0) + 1 INTO next_seq                                                     │
     │     FROM workflow_events                                                                                    │
     │     WHERE workflow_id = p_workflow_id;                                                                      │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     RETURN next_seq;                                                                                        │
     │ END;                                                                                                        │
     │ $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;                                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Files:                                                                                                      │

> Review this feedback from Co-Developer-1.  Re-evaluate your plan and incorporate what has merit in achieving 
the objectives.  Disagreement in the spirit of improvements is always welcome.  
<Co-Developer-1>
Review

  - [BLOCK] Event persistence isn’t atomic with state transitions: the plan
    moves SSE emission to an EventService, but unless calls are made inside the
    same DB transaction as snapshot/state_version updates, it violates Contract
    §11.3 (event + snapshot + version must be atomic). Current flow in apps/api/
    routes/workflows.py commits then emits; the plan doesn’t address that
    coupling.
  - [BLOCK] Replay correctness risk remains: removing broker backlog without
    default from_sequence=0 or updating tests breaks Gate E’s “connect after
    create” scenario. Also, replay→subscribe has a race where events committed
    between DB fetch and broker subscribe can be dropped unless you re-check
    sequence boundaries.
  - [BLOCK] Optimistic concurrency still incomplete as written:
    expected_position is added, but plan doesn’t confirm required validation
    against step_name (if required by Contract), and missing fields should be
    enforced by schema (not just optional logic). Ensure strict required fields
    (and validation errors) in apps/api/routes/workflows.py.
  - [BLOCK] Staleness propagation design doesn’t match spec: Contract/tech spec
    require staleness propagation on artifact INSERT/new revision, not only
    UPDATE is_stale. The proposed trigger in infra/db/migrations/
    versions/002_contract_alignment.py won’t fire unless artifacts are
    explicitly marked stale elsewhere.
  - [HIGH] Migration strategy: modifying infra/db/migrations/
    versions/002_contract_alignment.py after it may have been applied will break
    existing DBs. This should be a new migration (e.g., 003) rather than editing
    002.
  - [HIGH] state_version increment rule in Fix 7 is too narrow. Limiting
    increments to position changes will skip in_progress→awaiting_review
    transitions on the same step, which still affect action eligibility and must
    bump state_version (Contract §9.1/§11.3). apps/api/application/
    workflow_service.py needs a broader “state changed” check.
  - [HIGH] Progress endpoint still needs deterministic updated_at semantics:
    adding updated_at to step_executions without setting it on updates (or DB
    triggers/onupdate) will leave stale timestamps. This affects §10.3
    expectations (apps/api/infrastructure/db/models/step_execution.py, apps/api/
    routes/workflows.py).
  - [MEDIUM] SSE payloads must still include event_id (Gate E test checks this).
    The new EventService payload example omits it, which will regress apps/api/
    tests/e2e/test_gate_e.py.
  - [MEDIUM] Staleness schema names diverge from spec (stale vs is_stale,
    validated_at missing on traceability_links). Unless you add explicit mapping
    and document it, this is a spec mismatch (docs/Issued/Use-
    202601041305/4_SOLVER-technical-spec_V2_7_3.md).

  Recommendation: REVISE
  The plan is on the right path, but it still misses atomicity guarantees,
  replay race handling, and spec‑accurate staleness propagation. I recommend
  adjusting the plan to:

  1. ensure event persistence happens inside the same transaction as state
     updates;
  2. guarantee replay correctness (default from_sequence=0 or test updates +
     race fix);
  3. align staleness triggers to INSERT/new revision;
  4. use a new migration rather than modifying 002;
  5. broaden state_version increments to all state‑eligibility changes.


</Co-Developer-1>

⏺ The plan has been updated to incorporate all of Co-Developer-1's feedback from both reviews. Key changes made:

  1. Atomicity (Issue 14): Event persistence now happens INSIDE the same transaction as state updates, with broadcast via on_commit callback
  2. Replay race (Issue 15): Added sequence boundary re-check after broker subscribe to prevent dropped events
  3. Staleness trigger (Issue 16): Changed from AFTER UPDATE OF is_stale to AFTER INSERT when revision > 1
  4. Migration strategy (Issue 17): All changes now in NEW 003_remediation.py, not modifying 002
  5. state_version rule (Issue 18): Broadened to increment on ALL state-eligibility changes (position, status, artifacts, completion)
  6. event_id (Issue 19): Added UUID column for client-side deduplication
  7. updated_at trigger (Issue 20): DB trigger handles auto-update, not SQLAlchemy
  8. step_name validation (Issue 21): ExpectedPosition now includes step_name

     │ Remediation Plan                                                                                            │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Fix 1: Atomic Event Persistence (Issues 1, 2, 3, 14, 15, 19)                                                │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Problem: Events emitted via emit_event() in routes bypass persistence. Events are emitted AFTER transaction │
     │ commits, causing atomicity violations. Replay has race condition.                                           │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Solution: Move event persistence INTO workflow_service methods (same transaction). Broadcast happens after  │
     │ commit via deferred callback.                                                                               │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Changes:                                                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ 1. Update workflow_service.py action methods (approve_step, revise_step, etc.):                             │
     │   - Persist events INSIDE the same transaction as state updates                                             │
     │   - Use session.after_commit() callback to broadcast AFTER commit succeeds                                  │
     │ async def approve_step(self, ...):                                                                          │
     │     async with self._session.begin():  # Transaction boundary                                               │
     │         # ... perform state updates ...                                                                     │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         # Persist event IN transaction (atomicity)                                                          │
     │         event = await self._event_repo.create_event(                                                        │
     │             workflow_id=workflow.id,                                                                        │
     │             event_type="step_approved",                                                                     │
     │             payload={...},                                                                                  │
     │         )                                                                                                   │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │         # Schedule broadcast for AFTER commit (not inside txn)                                              │
     │         def broadcast_after_commit():                                                                       │
     │             asyncio.create_task(                                                                            │
     │                 self._broker.publish(workflow.workflow_id, SSEEvent(...))                                   │
     │             )                                                                                               │
     │         self._session.on_commit(broadcast_after_commit)                                                     │
     │ 2. Fix replay race condition (stream endpoint):                                                             │
     │   - Record from_sequence BEFORE subscribing to broker                                                       │
     │   - After broker subscribe, re-query DB for events > from_sequence                                          │
     │   - Deduplicate based on sequence number                                                                    │
     │ # 1. Record latest sequence BEFORE subscribing                                                              │
     │ max_seq = await event_repo.get_max_sequence(workflow_id)                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ # 2. Subscribe to broker                                                                                    │
     │ queue = broker.subscribe(workflow_id)                                                                       │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ # 3. Replay from requested sequence                                                                         │
     │ events = await event_repo.list_events(workflow_id, from_sequence=from_seq)                                  │
     │ for event in events:                                                                                        │
     │     yield event.to_sse_dict(workflow_id_str=workflow.workflow_id)                                           │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ # 4. Re-check for events committed during subscription                                                      │
     │ new_events = await event_repo.list_events(workflow_id, from_sequence=max_seq + 1)                           │
     │ seen_sequences = {e.sequence for e in events}                                                               │
     │ for event in new_events:                                                                                    │
     │     if event.sequence not in seen_sequences:                                                                │
     │         yield event.to_sse_dict(...)                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ # 5. Live events from broker (already have sequences)                                                       │
     │ async for event in queue:                                                                                   │
     │     yield event  # Already formatted with sequence                                                          │
     │ 3. Add event_id to SSE payloads:                                                                            │
     │   - Include event_id (UUID) in all SSE payloads for client-side deduplication                               │
     │   - Update WorkflowEvent model to include event_id column                                                   │
     │   - Update to_sse_dict() to include event_id                                                                │
     │ 4. Update to_sse_dict() to accept external workflow_id:                                                     │

     │ Fix 3: Full Staleness Tracking (Issues 6, 7, 8, 16, 17)                                                     │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Problem: Staleness only on artifacts, no traceability_links, no propagation, incomplete response. Trigger on│
     │  UPDATE (wrong) instead of INSERT (new revision).                                                           │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Solution: Add staleness to links + correct DB trigger + complete response schema. Use NEW 003 migration (not│
     │  modify 002).                                                                                               │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ Changes:                                                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ 1. Create NEW migration 003_remediation.py (DO NOT modify 002):                                             │
     │ -- Add staleness to traceability_links                                                                      │
     │ ALTER TABLE traceability_links                                                                              │
     │ ADD COLUMN is_stale BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,                                                         │
     │ ADD COLUMN stale_reason VARCHAR(500) NULL,                                                                  │
     │ ADD COLUMN stale_since TIMESTAMPTZ NULL;                                                                    │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ -- Fix advisory lock to use hashtext (collision-resistant)                                                  │
     │ CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_next_event_sequence(p_workflow_id UUID)                                      │
     │ RETURNS INTEGER AS $$                                                                                       │
     │ DECLARE                                                                                                     │
     │     next_seq INTEGER;                                                                                       │
     │     lock_id BIGINT;                                                                                         │
     │ BEGIN                                                                                                       │
     │     lock_id := hashtext(p_workflow_id::text)::bigint;                                                       │
     │     PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(lock_id);                                                                 │
     │     SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sequence), 0) + 1 INTO next_seq                                                     │
     │     FROM workflow_events WHERE workflow_id = p_workflow_id;                                                 │
     │     RETURN next_seq;                                                                                        │
     │ END;                                                                                                        │
     │ $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;                                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ -- Staleness propagation trigger: fire on INSERT (new revision)                                             │
     │ -- When a new artifact revision is created, mark EARLIER revisions as stale                                 │
     │ CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION propagate_staleness_on_new_revision()                                            │
     │ RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$                                                                                       │
     │ BEGIN                                                                                                       │
     │     -- When new artifact revision inserted, mark older revisions as stale                                   │
     │     UPDATE artifacts                                                                                        │
     │     SET is_stale = TRUE,                                                                                    │
     │         stale_reason = 'Superseded by revision ' || NEW.revision,                                           │
     │         stale_since = NOW()                                                                                 │
     │     WHERE workflow_id = NEW.workflow_id                                                                     │
     │       AND step_name = NEW.step_name                                                                         │
     │       AND artifact_type = NEW.artifact_type                                                                 │
     │       AND revision < NEW.revision                                                                           │
     │       AND is_stale = FALSE;                                                                                 │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     -- Also mark dependent traceability links as stale                                                      │
     │     UPDATE traceability_links                                                                               │
     │     SET is_stale = TRUE,                                                                                    │
     │         stale_reason = 'Source artifact superseded by revision ' || NEW.revision,                           │
     │         stale_since = NOW()                                                                                 │
     │     WHERE workflow_id = NEW.workflow_id                                                                     │
     │       AND from_step_number = NEW.step_number                                                                │
     │       AND is_stale = FALSE;                                                                                 │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │     RETURN NEW;                                                                                             │
     │ END;                                                                                                        │
     │ $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;                                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ CREATE TRIGGER artifact_new_revision_staleness                                                              │
     │ AFTER INSERT ON artifacts                                                                                   │
     │ FOR EACH ROW                                                                                                │
     │ WHEN (NEW.revision > 1)  -- Only trigger for revision > 1                                                   │
     │ EXECUTE FUNCTION propagate_staleness_on_new_revision();                                                     │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ -- Add event_id column for client-side deduplication                                                        │
     │ ALTER TABLE workflow_events                                                                                 │
     │ ADD COLUMN event_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid();                                                │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ -- Add updated_at to step_executions with auto-update trigger                                               │
     │ ALTER TABLE step_executions                                                                                 │
     │ ADD COLUMN updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW();                                                   │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_step_execution_timestamp()                                                │
     │ RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$                                                                                       │
     │ BEGIN                                                                                                       │
     │     NEW.updated_at = NOW();                                                                                 │
     │     RETURN NEW;                                                                                             │
     │ END;                                                                                                        │
     │ $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;                                                                                        │
     │                                                                                                             │
     │ CREATE TRIGGER step_execution_updated_at                                                                    │
     │ BEFORE UPDATE ON step_executions                                                                            │
     │ FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_step_execution_timestamp();                                            │
